# New Super Mario Bros. DS - Complete Check List

Every check is listed here with the exact name used by the client and tracker. Use the [Checks & Logic Guide](checks_logic.md) to find its level entrance, map route, signs, and Keys.

There are **1,618 possible checks** with every category and the World 6-2 bonus room enabled. Your game may use fewer: Toad Houses and Red Coin Challenges are on by default; Secret Exits, 1-Up Blocks, Blocksanity, and the bonus room are optional.

## How to use this list

Choose a world below, then search for the check name shown in your client or tracker.

- **Extra form** shows a Power-Up needed at the check itself. You need its Permit too if that license is enabled.
- **Large Mario** means Mushroom, Fire Flower, Blue Shell, or Mega Mushroom.
- **No extra form** still means you need the level or house unlocked. Its route may have its own requirements.
- **Advanced** marks a hidden or harder check covered by Advanced Location Item Placement.
- With shuffled levels, names follow the original course content. Use `/level` in the client to find its new entrance. 

For how each check is completed, see [Check types](checks_logic.md#check-types).

| Check type | Maximum checks, including the bonus room |
|---|---|
| Level Goal | 80 |
| Star Coin | 240 |
| Red Coin Challenge | 30 |
| Secret Exit | 18 |
| Boss | 9 |
| Toad House | 34 |
| 1-Up Block | 51 |
| Blocksanity | 1156 |

<summary>Technical location IDs and block positions</summary>

These details are optional and are intended for tracker maintenance, mapping, and troubleshooting.

**AP ID** is the unique location ID in hexadecimal. Block positions use the original course's global area ID and editor tile coordinates, with Y increasing downward. Each tile is 16 pixels; flying blocks show their starting position. The runtime conversion is `runtime_y = -editor_y - 1`. Coordinates identify a block rather than describe a route to it.
